# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 006 INDEPENDENCE WAVE GENERIC FOCUS FRAMEWORK
# ============================================================================
# This is the one shared generic tree granted to every admitted Event 006
# release. The full tree is loaded only through the explicit assignment effect
# for an Event 6-created package or a registered tag whose vanilla generic-tree
# status was explicitly reviewed. Existing meaningful trees are never replaced;
# their reviewed carrier overlays use the same shared route/effect contract.
# Regional and package modules below are gated nodes inside this tree, not
# separate country focus trees. No Event 006 package owns a second tree.
#
# Full-tree lanes:
# - survival and state construction
# - seven package-gated government settlements, including the Saar's distinct
#   municipal neutral commission
# - economy and regional infrastructure
# - army and researched military identity
# - diplomacy, recognition, and patrons
# - former-host settlement and regional ambition
# - independence network, league, formable, signature, and high-chaos work
# - IW-044 Tatarstan's river compact and Volga corridor package group
# - IW-038 Ruthenia, IW-045 Bashkiria and IW-070/071/072 Transcaucasus package
#   groups, each with its paid projects as mutually exclusive alternatives
# ============================================================================

# IW-012 Iceland route consumers. The vanilla Iceland tree imports the shared
# overlay through an exact snapshot carrier, so these four choices are visible
# without replacing any vanilla focus block. Each route is gated by its own
# concrete project, ledger, and threshold checks before it can lock the shared
# Event 006 government route.

shared_focus = {
	id = independence_wave_ice_choose_constitutional_route
	icon = GFX_goal_independence_wave_constitutional_state
	prerequisite = { focus = independence_wave_overlay_open_regional_ambition }
	mutually_exclusive = {
		focus = independence_wave_ice_restore_traditional_authority
		focus = independence_wave_ice_establish_emergency_command
		focus = independence_wave_ice_accept_patron_mandate
	}
	relative_position_id = independence_wave_overlay_take_stock_of_independence
	x = -3
	y = 4
	cost = @independence_wave_focus_standard
	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }
	allow_branch = { can_open_independence_wave_ice_route_focus = yes }
	available = { can_lock_independence_wave_ice_constitutional_route = yes }
	completion_reward = { custom_effect_tooltip = independence_wave_ice_choose_constitutional_route_tt hidden_effect = { independence_wave_ice_focus_lock_constitutional_route = yes } }
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.standard
		modifier = {
			factor = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.strong_preference_factor
			check_variable = { var = independence_wave_ice_civic_cohesion value = constant:independence_wave_ice_value.stable_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_independence_wave_severe_host_threat = yes }
			NOT = { has_war = yes }
		}
		modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.preferred_factor has_country_flag = independence_wave_ice_former_host_charter_settled }
		modifier = {
			factor = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.war_avoid_factor
			OR = { has_war = yes has_independence_wave_severe_host_threat = yes }
		}
		modifier = { factor = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.avoid_factor has_independence_wave_severe_instability = yes }
	}
}

	available = {
		can_use_independence_wave_tat_focus_group = yes
		has_completed_focus = independence_wave_complete_founding_settlement
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = independence_wave_tat_host_ledgers_focus_project_idle_tt
			NOT = { has_decision = independence_wave_tat_settle_former_host_ledgers }
		}
	}
	bypass = {
		can_use_independence_wave_tat_focus_group = yes
		has_country_flag = independence_wave_tat_host_ledgers_settled
	}
	completion_reward = {
		if = {
			limit = { can_use_independence_wave_tat_focus_group = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes
					var:independence_wave_former_host = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = independence_wave_tat_host_ledgers_effect_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes }
				custom_effect_tooltip = independence_wave_tat_host_ledgers_at_war_effect_tt
			}
			else = { custom_effect_tooltip = independence_wave_tat_host_loss_effect_tt }
			hidden_effect = { independence_wave_tat_focus_settle_former_host_ledgers = yes }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.high
		modifier = {
			factor = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.preferred_factor
			has_independence_wave_tat_route_government = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.preferred_factor
			has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes
			NOT = { has_independence_wave_severe_host_threat = yes }
		}
	}
}

			tooltip = independence_wave_bsk_host_ledgers_focus_project_idle_tt
			NOT = { has_decision = independence_wave_bsk_settle_former_host_ledgers }
		}
	}
	bypass = {
		can_use_independence_wave_bsk_focus_group = yes
		has_country_flag = independence_wave_bsk_host_ledgers_settled
	}
	completion_reward = {
		if = {
			limit = { can_use_independence_wave_bsk_focus_group = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes
					var:independence_wave_former_host = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = independence_wave_bsk_host_ledgers_effect_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes }
				custom_effect_tooltip = independence_wave_bsk_host_ledgers_at_war_effect_tt
			}
			else = { custom_effect_tooltip = independence_wave_bsk_host_loss_effect_tt }
			hidden_effect = { independence_wave_bsk_focus_settle_former_host_ledgers = yes }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.high
		modifier = {
			factor = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.preferred_factor
			has_independence_wave_bsk_route_government = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.preferred_factor
			has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes
			NOT = { has_independence_wave_severe_host_threat = yes }
		}
	}
}

	available = {
		can_use_independence_wave_azr_focus_group = yes
		custom_trigger_tooltip = {
			tooltip = independence_wave_azr_civic_focus_project_idle_tt
			NOT = { has_decision = independence_wave_azr_restore_caspian_rail }
		}
	}
	bypass = {
		can_use_independence_wave_azr_focus_group = yes
		has_country_flag = independence_wave_iw072_caspian_rail_restored
	}
	completion_reward = {
		if = {
			limit = { can_use_independence_wave_azr_focus_group = yes }
			custom_effect_tooltip = independence_wave_azr_rail_focus_effect_tt
			hidden_effect = { independence_wave_iw072_complete_rail = yes }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.high
		modifier = {
			factor = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.strong_preference_factor
			has_active_mission = independence_wave_azr_secure_baku_oil_belt
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.preferred_factor
			OR = { has_independence_wave_constitutional_route = yes has_independence_wave_popular_council_route = yes }
		}
	}
}

shared_focus = {
	id = independence_wave_azr_settle_with_the_former_host
	icon = GFX_goal_independence_wave_former_host_settlement
	prerequisite = { focus = independence_wave_azr_reconnect_the_caspian_railway }
	prerequisite = { focus = independence_wave_azr_win_export_recognition }
	relative_position_id = independence_wave_azr_guard_the_oil_belt
	x = 0
	y = 2
	cost = @independence_wave_focus_standard
	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }
	allow_branch = { can_use_independence_wave_azr_focus_group = yes }
	available = {
		can_use_independence_wave_azr_focus_group = yes
		has_completed_focus = independence_wave_complete_founding_settlement
	}
	bypass = {
		can_use_independence_wave_azr_focus_group = yes
		has_country_flag = independence_wave_transcaucasus_host_settlement_focus_complete
	}
	completion_reward = {
		if = {
			limit = { can_use_independence_wave_azr_focus_group = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes
					var:independence_wave_former_host = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = independence_wave_transcaucasus_host_settlement_focus_effect_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes }
				custom_effect_tooltip = independence_wave_transcaucasus_host_settlement_at_war_focus_effect_tt
			}
			else = { custom_effect_tooltip = independence_wave_transcaucasus_host_settlement_loss_focus_effect_tt }
			hidden_effect = { independence_wave_transcaucasus_focus_settle_former_host = yes }
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		base = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.high
		modifier = {
			factor = constant:independence_wave_focus_ai.preferred_factor
			has_independence_wave_living_former_host = yes
			NOT = { has_independence_wave_severe_host_threat = yes }
		}
	}
}
